!python /kaggle/input/datasets/ashwider/12pipeline/kaggle/input/datasets/kagglemister122/12pipeline/eeg_preprocessing_core.py \
    --dataset physionet \
    --root /kaggle/input/datasets/ashwider/dataset/files \
    --output /kaggle/working/122preprocessed \
    --channel-set all \
    --task-filter mi_hand \
    --band-method fft_segments \
    --band-segments 8 \
    --save-mode all \
    --window-sec 2.0 \
    --low 4.0 \
    --high 38.0 \
    --seed 42
high cutoff 38.0 Hz is above beta range while --include-gamma is disabled. For tighter cross-view spectral alignment, use --high 30.0 or enable --include-gamma.
